Initial curated pass. Book initial curated pass complete. Overall next: None. Greek-based Saints-Translation numbering is retained.

Completed means the initial curated cross-reference pass: the source verse and every listed target passage were read in the pinned Saints-Translation English records, and each retained pair has a specific explanation. It does not mean every possible biblical connection has been exhausted, or independent scholarly approval.

2 Samuel 1:1

After Saul died, David returned from striking Amalek. David stayed in Ziklag for two days.

  1. 1 Samuel 30:17-19 David's return after the Amalek recovery

    The opening places David at Ziklag after striking Amalek; the earlier account describes his strike and recovery of the captives and goods, without independently calculating every intervening day.

    Read matching passage

    1 Samuel 30:17 David came upon them and struck them from daybreak until evening, and on the following day. No man among them was saved except four hundred youths who had mounted camels and fled.

    1 Samuel 30:18 David recovered everything the Amalekites had taken, and he rescued both his wives.

    1 Samuel 30:19 Nothing belonging to them was missing, from small to great, from the spoils to sons and daughters and everything they had taken from them. David brought it all back.
